We propose the Structured Dynamics Model (SDM), which explicitly separates the dominant source of temporal change from residual dynamics through future-feature prediction, rather than representing video change with a single entangled latent or with unstructured, spatially dense transition tokens. Training combines self-supervised learning on real video with weak supervision of scene dynamics on synthetic Kubric data. We evaluate SDM on ProbeMotion, a new evaluation suite spanning synthetic and real videos with camera motion, object motion, and combined dynamics. SDM outperforms backbone baselines using global CLS or average-pooled features, and compares favorably to strongly supervised representations such as VGGT on several probes, despite using substantially weaker supervision. These results suggest that pretrained image models can be readily repurposed into structured video-dynamics representations, providing a useful inductive bias for learning and analyzing latent video dynamics.

</details>


### [89] [Synthetic data generation framework for quality control automation in gravure printing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21577)
*Korota Arsène Coulibaly,Mohamed Hamlich,Khalid Hmali,Andrea Trombin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对凹版印刷质量控制的合成数据生成框架，自动生成高保真印刷缺陷图像及标注，解决了真实工业缺陷数据稀缺的问题。使用生成的7533张图像训练RFDETR模型，在真实工业测试样本上达到80.9%的mAP，为零成本、快速部署的自动化缺陷检测提供了解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹版印刷中的质量控制仍依赖缓慢、昂贵且主观的人工检测。虽然深度学习模型有望实现自动化，但训练鲁棒的模型（如YOLO、Vision Transformers）严重受限于真实工业缺陷图像的极端稀缺性。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的合成数据生成框架，自动生成凹版印刷特定缺陷（褶皱、条纹、套印不准等）的高保真图像，并输出对应的边界框和标注。使用生成的7533张合成图像训练最先进的目标检测模型RFDETR。

Result: 在真实工业测试样本上，使用合成数据训练的模型实现了80.9%的平均精度均值（mAP）。

Conclusion: 该框架为零成本、快速部署的解决方案，可自动化印刷生产线的缺陷检测，无需大规模人工数据采集。

Abstract: Quality control in printing, particularly in rotogravure printing, still depends on slow, costly, and subjective manual inspection. Automated surface defect detection is critical for maintaining high-quality standards in rotogravure printing. Deep learning models give prospects for automation. However, training robust deep learning models, such as YOLO or Vision Transformers, is heavily hindered by the extreme scarcity of real-world industrial defects images. To overcome this limitation, this paper introduces a novel synthetic data generation framework tailored for rotogravure printing quality control. The proposed pipeline automatically generates high-fidelity images of specific printing defects (creases, streaks, misregistration, etc.) and outputs corresponding bounding boxes and annotations. To validate the framework, a synthetic dataset of 7533 images was generated and used to train the state-of-the-art object-detection model RFDETR. Experimental results demonstrate that the model trained on our synthetic data achieves a Mean Average Precision (mAP) of 80.9\% on real industrial testing samples. This framework provides a zero-cost, rapid-deployment solution for automating defect inspection in printing lines without requiring massive manual data collection.

</details>


### [90] [GraphVid: Interactive Graph-Controllable Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21580)
*Vedant Shah,Onkar Susladkar,Tushar Prakash,Kiet Nguyen,Tianjio Yu,Adheesh Juvekar,Muntasir Waheed,Ismini Lourentzou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphVid是一种基于图条件的图像到视频生成模型，通过结构化交互图实现可控的多主体视频生成，同时发布了包含关系注释的大规模交互视频数据集GraphVid-Bench。相比Motion-I2V，GraphVid在FID上降低39.9%，FVD降低37.6%，PSNR从9.87提升至15.98，SSIM从0.38提升至0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可控视频生成方法在指定多物体交互时存在困难：文本提示难以精确描述复杂交互，轨迹控制需要用户为多个物体绘制精确轨迹，在场景复杂或遮挡情况下表现不佳且扩展性差。

Method: 提出GraphVid模型，采用图条件（graph-conditioned）控制方式，通过结构化交互图实现灵活而精确的多主体控制；同时构建GraphVid-Bench大规模交互视频数据集，提供结构化关系标注以支持交互感知视频生成模型的训练。

Result: 相比Motion-I2V，GraphVid的FID降低39.9%，FVD降低37.6%，PSNR从9.87提升至15.98，SSIM从0.38提升至0.61，且使用了更少的训练数据和可训练参数。

Conclusion: 结构化语义接口是一种有潜力的可控视频生成范式，能够在数据量和参数量更少的情况下实现更强的可控性和视频质量。

Abstract: Controllable video generation remains challenging due to the difficulty of specifying precise multi-object interactions using text prompts or motion-control inputs that primarily constrain pixel movement. In practice, trajectory-based control often requires users to draw accurate tracks for multiple objects, which scales poorly with scene complexity and becomes ambiguous under occlusion or overlap. To enable flexible yet precise multi-subject control, we introduce $\textbf{GraphVid}$, a graph-conditioned image-to-video generation model that enables interactive control through structured interaction graphs. We further curate $\textbf{GraphVid-Bench}$, a large-scale interaction-centric video dataset with structured relational annotations to enable training of interaction-aware video generation models. Despite using substantially less training data and fewer trainable parameters than prior motion-control methods, GraphVid delivers strong controllability and video quality. Compared with Motion-I2V, GraphVid reduces FID by up to 39.9% and FVD by 37.6%, while improving PSNR (9.87=>15.98) and SSIM (0.38=>0.61). Our results highlight the potential of structured semantic interfaces as a powerful paradigm for controllable video generation.

</details>


### [91] [Inference-Time Scaling of Diffusion Models via Progressive Seed Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21591)
*Rogerio Guimaraes,Pietro Perona*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式种子剪枝（PSP）方法，通过在推理前期探索多个噪声种子并激进剪枝，在不增加总模型评估次数的情况下，利用固定计算预算更有效地提升扩散模型和流匹配模型的图像生成质量，在奖励引导选择和提示对齐方面优于Best-of-N、重要性采样和树搜索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配模型在条件图像生成中占据主导地位，但其推理时缩放（inference-time scaling）远不如自回归语言模型成熟。由于最终质量对初始噪声种子高度敏感，现有方法通常采用黑盒奖励下的种子搜索或重采样来消耗额外计算，但在整个推理过程中保持恒定的内存占用。本文旨在放松这一约束，探索一种未被充分利用的推理时缩放轴。

Method: 提出渐进式种子剪枝（Progressive Seed Pruning, PSP），在推理前期前载探索（front-loading exploration），评估大量种子并激进剪枝。具体通过对中间去噪估计进行评分，逐步缩小候选集，只保留有潜力的轨迹进行完整去噪，同时保持总模型评估次数不变。

Result: 在扩散和流匹配骨干网络上，PSP在奖励引导选择上持续提升，并在GenEval评分（自动评估）和人类评估的提示对齐方面，在相同计算量下优于Best-of-N、重要性采样和树搜索基线。

Conclusion: 放松推理过程中内存占用恒定的约束，通过前期探索和渐进剪枝策略，可以在固定计算预算下更有效地提升生成质量，为扩散模型的推理时缩放提供了新方向。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching models dominate conditional image generation, yet inference-time scaling for these models is far less developed than for autoregressive language models. Because final quality is highly sensitive to the initial noise seed, many approaches spend extra compute on seed search or resampling under a black-box reward, but typically maintaining a constant memory footprint throughout inference. We show that relaxing this constraint enables an underexplored inference-time scaling axis: by front-loading exploration, evaluating many seeds early, and pruning aggressively, we can use a fixed compute budget more effectively. \emph{Progressive Seed Pruning} (\PSP) scores intermediate denoised estimates and progressively narrows the candidate set so that only promising trajectories are fully denoised, while keeping the total number of model evaluations fixed. Across diffusion and flow-matching backbones, \PSP \ consistently improves reward-guided selection and achieves higher GenEval scores (automated) and better human evaluation on prompt-alignment than best-of-$N$, importance-sampling, and tree-search baselines at matched compute. Project page: https://www.vision.caltech.edu/psp. Code: https://github.com/rogerioagjr/psp.

</details>


### [92] [Unified Video Dense Prediction from Disjoint Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21592)
*Yihong Sun,Seoung Wug Oh,Jiahui Huang,Bharath Hariharan,Joon-Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniD是一个统一的视频模型，通过从独立、领域特定的数据集中联合学习八种密集场景属性（几何、外观、语义），利用预训练扩散模型的视觉先验和轻量级任务投影器进行知识蒸馏，无需标注重叠或伪标签，实现了与单任务专家相当的性能和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景理解方法受限于任务特定标注的碎片化问题，不同领域数据集之间不兼容；当前统一系统要么限制使用完全共同标注的数据，要么承担伪标签的大计算成本，缺乏高效且可扩展的统一解决方案。

Method: 提出UniD统一视频模型，通过蒸馏步骤让每个任务的专家模型通过轻量级任务投影器监督统一骨干网络，利用预训练扩散模型的强视觉先验来弥合不同训练数据源之间的领域差距，无需标注重叠或伪标签。

Result: UniD在八个密集预测任务上取得了与单任务专家和多任务基线相当的性能，展现出对分布外场景的强泛化能力，以及增强的时间一致性和跨任务一致性。

Conclusion: 预训练扩散模型的视觉先验足以弥合不同领域数据源之间的差距，使得从碎片化标注数据中学习统一的多任务模型成为可能，为无需共同标注的统一场景理解提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Scene understanding requires simultaneous prediction about geometry, appearance, and semantics. However, existing task-specific annotations are fragmented across incompatible, domain-specific datasets. Current unified systems circumvent this by restricting training to fully co-annotated data, or by incurring the large computational cost of pseudo-labeling. To mitigate this, we introduce UniD, a unified video model that jointly predicts eight dense scene properties-depth, surface normals, semantic segmentation, boundaries, human parts, albedo, shading, and materials-all learned from disjoint, domain-specific datasets. We propose a simple yet effective distillation step in which per-task experts supervise a unified backbone through lightweight task projectors, eliminating the need for annotation overlap or pseudo-labeling. Our key insight is that the strong visual priors of a pretrained diffusion model are sufficient to bridge the domain gaps introduced by disjoint training sources, enabling robust generalization to scene-task combinations never seen during training. UniD achieves competitive performance against per-task specialists and multi-task baselines, with strong generalization to out-of-distribution scenarios and enhanced temporal and cross-task consistency. Code and video results are available at https://unid-video.github.io/.

</details>


### [93] [Streaming Multi-Agent Autoregressive Diffusion Model with World State Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21594)
*Sicheng Mo,Yuheng Li,Ziyang Leng,Krishna Kumar Singh,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldWeaver (W²) 是一种流式多智能体视频扩散模型，通过引入可学习的跨智能体世界状态寄存器来维护共享世界信息，并在双智能体Minecraft视频生成中提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归视频扩散模型仅传递观察历史作为条件上下文，导致在多智能体和多视图场景中难以维护共享的世界状态。本文旨在解决这一缺陷，提出显式的世界状态建模方法。

Method: 提出WorldWeaver模型：(1) 引入可学习的跨智能体世界状态寄存器，存储共享世界信息并追踪个体智能体状态，在生成每个片段后动态更新；(2) 使用个体智能体状态、全局视图（包括鸟瞰图）和场景文本作为监督信号；(3) 采用Mixture-of-Transformers架构，分别用独立权重处理世界状态建模和视觉帧建模。

Result: 在双智能体Minecraft视频生成任务中，显式的世界状态建模显著提升了逻辑一致性和生成质量。

Conclusion: 显式的世界状态建模是改善多智能体视频生成中逻辑一致性的有效方法，WorldWeaver为此提供了可行的架构方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent interactive world models should not only generate consistent observations, but also maintain world states that persist across agents and evolve across views. Existing autoregressive video diffusion pipelines carry forward observation history as conditioning context, which makes shared state difficult to maintain in multi-agent and multi-view settings. We present WorldWeaver (W^2), a streaming multi-agent video diffusion model that augments rollout with cross-agent world state registers: learnable tokens that store shared world information, track individual agent status, and are dynamically updated after each generated chunk. We ground these registers with supervision signals spanning individual agent status, global state views including bird's-eye views, and scene text. We further improve the architecture with a Mixture-of-Transformers design that uses separate weights for world state modeling and visual frame modeling. Extensive experiments in two-agent Minecraft video generation show that explicit world-state modeling improves logical consistency and generation quality.

</details>


### [94] [3D-Aware VLMs with Implicit and Explicit Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21595)
*Wenhao Li,Xueying Jiang,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Ran Xu,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLM-IE3D是一个统一框架，通过从RGB视频中学习的隐式和显式3D几何表征（IGTs和EGTs），增强视觉语言模型（VLMs）的3D空间感知能力，在多种3D任务上取得优越性能，且无需额外3D输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于2D视觉输入的视觉语言模型在处理需要细粒度空间理解的3D任务时表现不佳，缺乏3D空间感知能力。

Method: 提出VLM-IE3D框架，包含：(1) 隐式几何令牌（IGTs）从视频中捕获高层几何先验；(2) 显式几何令牌（EGTs）从重建的3D属性中编码详细几何结构；(3) 3D感知适配器融合两类几何表征与2D视觉线索。仅需RGB输入即可注入强3D归纳偏置。

Result: 在3D视频检测、3D视觉定位、3D密集字幕生成和空间推理等多个3D任务上一致取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 仅利用RGB视频输入结合隐式和显式3D几何表征，能有效提升VLMs的细粒度3D空间理解与推理能力，且无需额外3D传感器输入。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, most existing vision-language models (VLMs) built from 2D visual inputs often struggle when handling various 3D tasks that require fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning. To bridge this gap, we present VLM-IE3D, a unified framework that enhances the 3D spatial awareness of VLMs by equipping them with both implicit and explicit 3D geometries learned from RGB videos. Our VLM-IE3D introduces Implicit Geometry Tokens (IGTs) that capture high-level geometric priors from input videos, as well as complementary Explicit Geometry Tokens (EGTs) that encode detailed geometric structures from reconstructed 3D attributes. On top of that, VLM-IE3D comes with a 3D-aware adapter that effectively fuses the two types of geometric representations with 2D visual cues. This RGB-only design injects strong 3D inductive biases for fine-grained spatial understanding and reasoning without requiring any additional 3D inputs. Extensive experiments show that VLM-IE3D achieves superior performance consistently across various 3D tasks including 3D video detection, 3D visual grounding, 3D dense captioning, and spatial reasoning. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Vegetebird/VLM-IE3D.

</details>


### [95] [Deblurring in the Wild: A Real-World Image Deblurring Dataset from Smartphone High-Speed Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19445)
*Syed Mumtahin Mahmud,Mahdi Mohd Hossain Noki,Prothito Shovon Majumder,Abdul Mohaimen Al Radi,Sudipto Das Sukanto,Afia Lubaina,Md. Mosaddek Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了目前最大的真实世界图像去模糊数据集，利用智能手机慢动作视频（240帧/秒）构建了超过42,000对高分辨率模糊-清晰图像对，规模约为现有数据集的10倍。通过平均帧模拟长曝光模糊，以中心帧作为清晰参考。多个SOTA模型在该基准上性能显著下降，表明其作为更具挑战性和多样性的去模糊新基准的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像去模糊数据集规模有限、场景多样性不足，难以支撑鲁棒且泛化的去模糊模型训练与评估，亟需更大规模、更贴近真实世界的去模糊基准。

Method: 利用智能手机慢动作视频（240 fps）采集数据，通过平均连续帧模拟长曝光模糊，以时间中心帧作为清晰参考图，构建模糊-清晰图像对。数据集包含42,000+对高分辨率图像，覆盖室内外多种场景及不同的物体与相机运动。

Result: 构建的数据集规模是广泛使用数据集的约10倍，场景数量为8倍。多个SOTA去模糊模型在该基准上性能显著下降，验证了数据集的复杂性与多样性。

Conclusion: 该数据集作为一个具有挑战性的新基准，有助于推动更鲁棒、更具泛化能力的去模糊模型的发展。

Abstract: We introduce the largest real-world image deblurring dataset constructed from smartphone slow-motion videos. Using 240 frames captured over one second, we simulate realistic long-exposure blur by averaging frames to produce blurry images, while using the temporally centered frame as the sharp reference. Our dataset contains over 42,000 high-resolution blur-sharp image pairs, making it approximately 10 times larger than widely used datasets, with 8 times the amount of different scenes, including indoor and outdoor environments, with varying object and camera motions. We benchmark multiple state-of-the-art (SOTA) deblurring models on our dataset and observe significant performance degradation, highlighting the complexity and diversity of our benchmark. Our dataset serves as a challenging new benchmark to facilitate robust and generalizable deblurring models.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [What is Good? Extracting and Testing Implicit Theories of Literary Quality from LLM Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20425)
*Birger Moëll*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过两项研究探讨了推理型大语言模型如何评估文学质量。研究1构建了涵盖六个质量层级（从经典文学到匿名论坛帖子）的30篇真实文本基准，发现DeepSeek模型达到79.3%的层级分类准确率，其推理轨迹揭示模型重视意图性而非正确性，优先考量技巧、深度和独特声音。研究2通过对五篇经典散文进行六种系统性降质操作（词汇简化、节奏扁平化、意象移除、声音泛化、结构简化及综合降质），发现词汇简化造成的质量损失最小（0.41分），而结构（2.78分）和声音（2.34分）损失更大，综合降质效果虽严重（-5.64分）但呈次可加性。研究表明LLM对写作质量的判断是整体性的、作者特定的，且对结构特征比词汇特征更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学研究与计算语言学中长期存在一个核心问题：是什么让写作变得"好"。作者希望系统探究推理型大语言模型对文学质量的评估机制，揭示其内隐的质量评判理论。

Method: 两项研究设计。研究1：构建30篇横跨6个质量层级的真实文本基准，通过5次DeepSeek复制实验进行层级分类，并从推理轨迹中提取模型的质量理论；同时进行风格匹配但不可识别的熟悉度实验。研究2：对5篇经典散文进行6种系统降质操作（词汇简化、节奏扁平化、意象移除、声音泛化、结构简化、综合降质），重新评估各版本质量。

Result: 研究1：模型达到79.3%的层级分类准确率，推理轨迹显示模型重视意图性、技巧、深度和独特声音；熟悉度实验中源识别可能虚高分数。研究2：词汇简化质量损失最小（0.41±0.46分），结构（2.78分）和声音（2.34分）损失更大，综合降质效果严重（-5.64分）但呈次可加性；与Qwen QwQ的探索性比较显示相同定性模式。

Conclusion: LLM对写作质量的判断是整体性的（holistic）、作者特定的（author-specific），对结构特征比词汇特征更敏感。这一发现对自动化写作反馈和计算美学具有重要启示。

Abstract: What makes writing "good" remains a persistent question in literary studies and computational linguistics. We present a two-study investigation of how reasoning-enabled LLMs evaluate literary quality.
  In Study 1, we construct a benchmark of 30 real texts spanning six quality tiers, from canonical literature to anonymous forum posts, and extract the model's implicit theory of quality from its reasoning traces. Across five DeepSeek replications, the model achieves 79.3% mean tier-classification accuracy. The traces reveal a consistent stated theory: the model values intentionality over correctness, prioritizing craft, depth, and distinctive voice. A familiarity experiment with style-matched but unrecognizable passages suggests that source recognition may inflate scores, although this is confounded by genuine quality differences between canonical originals and researcher-written pastiches.
  In Study 2, we probe this theory through systematic degradation of five canonical prose passages. We apply six manipulations - vocabulary simplification, rhythm flattening, imagery removal, voice genericization, structure simplification, and combined degradation - and reevaluate each version. Vocabulary simplification causes the smallest quality loss (0.41 +/- 0.46 points), far below structure (2.78) or voice (2.34) loss. Combined degradation is devastating (-5.64) but subadditive. An exploratory comparison with Qwen QwQ shows the same broad qualitative pattern.
  Together, these studies suggest that LLM judgments of writing quality are holistic, author-specific, and more sensitive to structural than lexical features, with implications for automated writing feedback and computational aesthetics.

</details>


### [97] [Knowledge Injection Exists in MoE? Exploring Expert-Aware Contrast Decoding in MoE for Mitigating LLMs'Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20426)
*Xinyue Fang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhen Huang,Ziyi Pan,Zhihua Wen,Xi Wang,Quntian Fang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有幻觉缓解方法（提示工程、模型优化）难以改变模型内部知识或跨域泛化差。对比解码利用层间差异，但此前仅关注Transformer模型。本文研究MoE模型中的层间差异，发现MoE（含共享专家）不存在类似差异，但高层专家激活模式在事实与非事实输出间不同。据此提出EAACD，一种基于专家感知的自适应对比解码方法，将MoE高层专家按置信度与一致性分为高可靠组和若干低可靠组，通过对比组间预测来校准原始预测，并通过注意力与掩码增强低可靠专家的幻觉作为负参考。EAACD在四个数据集上超越所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM幻觉缓解方法（提示工程、模型优化）要么难以改变模型内部知识，要么跨域泛化能力差。对比解码虽能利用层间差异缓解幻觉，但此前研究仅针对Transformer模型（如GPT），忽略了混合专家（MoE）模型。由于MoE改变了传统Transformer架构，有必要探究MoE中是否存在类似的层间差异，并据此设计专门的幻觉缓解方法。

Method: 提出EAACD（Expert-Aware Adaptive Contrast Decoding）。首先实证发现MoE（含共享专家）中不存在类似Transformer的层间差异，但高层专家在事实与非事实输出上激活模式不同。据此，将MoE高层专家按置信度与一致性分为一个高可靠组和多个低可靠组，通过对比高可靠组与各低可靠组的预测来校准原始预测。为强化对比，通过注意力与掩码机制放大低可靠专家的幻觉，提供更强的负参考信号。

Result: 在四个数据集上，EAACD在QA任务上的幻觉缓解效果显著优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: EAACD通过利用MoE模型高层专家的差异化激活模式进行自适应对比解码，有效缓解了幻觉问题，填补了MoE模型幻觉缓解研究的空白，并在多个QA数据集上取得最佳性能。

Abstract: Existing LLM hallucination mitigation methods, including prompt engineering and model optimization, either hardly alter models'internal knowledge or have poor cross-domain generalization. Contrastive decoding mitigates hallucinations by using layer-wise differences in LLMs. However, prior studies only explore transformer-based models (e.g., GPT), ignoring other effective frameworks like mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. Since MoE alters the traditional transformer architecture, we conduct empirical studies to investigate whether similar layer-wise differences exist in MoEs. Our results show that they do not exist in MoE with shared experts; nevertheless, across different MoEs, higher layers exhibit distinct expert activation patterns between factual and non-factual outputs. Building on these, we propose EAACD, an expert-aware adaptive contrast decoding that uses expert differences in MoE's higher layers to mitigate hallucinations on QA tasks. EAACD splits high-layer experts into a higher-reliability group and several lower-reliability groups based on their confidence and consistency. It contrasts the higher-reliability group's prediction with each lower-reliability group's prediction to calibrate the model's original predictions. To strengthen this contrast, EAACD amplifies hallucinations from lower-reliability experts via attention and masking to provide stronger negative references. EAACD outperforms all baselines on four datasets.

</details>


### [98] [Is MoE Routing a Huffman Code? Discovering the Frequency-Diversity Law in Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20427)
*Ching-Chieh Tsao,Zhuoyi Lin,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE路由机制本质上是哈夫曼编码的体现，提出“频率-多样性定律”，并发现负载均衡可能导致功能冗余，进而提出子集差异剪枝策略来消除冗余，释放模型的哈夫曼编码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构的路由机制是一个黑盒，缺乏对其底层原理的理解，需要揭示其背后的基本规律。

Method: 提出频率-多样性定律（Frequency-Diversity Law）解释路由机制；识别Qwen3.5-35B-A3B中的冗余陷阱；提出子集差异剪枝（Subset Difference Pruning）策略消除功能冗余。

Result: Phi-3.5-MoE和Gemma-4-27B-A4B等模型自发地作为信息论引擎运行；Qwen3.5-35B-A3B在低有效稀疏度下存在功能冗余；子集差异剪枝能够消除冗余而不损害推理能力，释放潜在哈夫曼效率。

Conclusion: 下一代MoE应从强制负载均衡转向最小描述长度（MDL）最优性，将路由从启发式方法转变为有原则的压缩引擎。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts architectures have revolutionized scaling, yet the underlying logic of their routing remains a black box. In this paper, we uncover a fundamental governing principle: MoE routing is not merely selection, but a manifestation of Huffman Coding. We introduce the Frequency-Diversity Law, revealing that state-of-the-art models, such as Phi-3.5-MoE and Gemma-4-27B-A4B, spontaneously act as information-theoretic engines. These models allocate sparse expert resources for common tokens while invoking high-diversity expert committees for rare, complex tasks found in chain-of-thought trajectories. However, we identify a critical redundancy trap in Qwen3.5-35B-A3B: when effective sparsity (k/E_eff) is sufficiently low, load-balancing inadvertently imposes functional redundancy, masking the underlying Huffman efficiency signal. To bridge this gap, we propose Subset Difference Pruning, a surgical strategy to eliminate functional duplicates. We demonstrate that pruning does not degrade reasoning; instead, it unleashes the model's latent Huffman efficiency, forcing the logic to collapse into streamlined, high-density paths. Our findings suggest that the next generation of MoEs should move beyond forced load-balancing toward Minimum Description Length (MDL) optimality, assigning shorter expert-routing codes to high-frequency information and longer, more diverse codes to low-frequency information, thereby transforming routing from a heuristic into a principled compression engine.

</details>


### [99] [Human-in-the-Loop Large Language Model Framework for Identification of Cutaneous Immune-Related Adverse Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20428)
*Charles Lu,Olivia Burke,Debby Cheng,Adam Kashlan,Caitlyn Duffy,Zeyun Lu,Lirit Fuksman,Jin Ning Tian,Andrew Sedlack,Priya Katyal,Eudora Lee,Ralina Karagenova,Chuck Lin,Kun-Hsing Yu,Nicole LeBoeuf,Alexander Gusev,Yevgeniy R. Semenov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个检索增强的多智能体大语言模型（LLM）驱动、人在回路的框架，用于从临床笔记中检测皮肤免疫相关不良事件（cirAEs）。与无辅助人工审核相比，LLM辅助工作流提升了准确率（F1=0.88 vs 0.77）、评分者间信度（Cohen's kappa=0.82 vs 0.50），并将平均审核时间缩短约一半。该框架展示了LLM在跨器官系统识别免疫相关毒性以及实现准确、可扩展、透明的不良事件数据提取方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工审核临床笔记以检测皮肤免疫相关不良事件（cirAEs）耗时且评分者间一致性较低，亟需一种高效、准确且可扩展的自动化方法来辅助这一过程。

Method: 采用检索增强的多智能体LLM驱动框架，并融入人在回路（human-in-the-loop）机制，从临床笔记中自动检测cirAEs。

Result: 与无辅助人工审核相比，LLM辅助工作流在F1分数（0.88 vs 0.77）、Cohen's kappa（0.82 vs 0.50）上均有提升，且平均审核时间缩短约一半。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了LLM在不良事件数据提取中的应用潜力，可跨器官系统推广，实现准确、可扩展且透明的免疫相关毒性检测。

Abstract: This study evaluated a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent large language model (LLM)-driven, human-in-the-loop framework for detecting cutaneous immune-related adverse events (cirAEs) from clinical notes. Compared with unassisted manual review, the LLM-assisted workflow improved accuracy (F1 = 0.88 vs 0.77), inter-rater agreement measured by Cohen's kappa (kappa = 0.82 vs 0.50), and reduced average review time by approximately half. This framework pilots how LLMs can be applied to identify immune-related toxicities across organ systems and, more broadly, enable accurate, scalable, and transparent adverse event data extraction.

</details>


### [100] [More Is Not More: What Matters for Diversity in LLM Opinions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20429)
*Qiyang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过析因实验系统研究了提升LLM输出多样性的两种干预维度（输入条件与交互架构），发现：人物角色细节并非越多越好；不同架构探索的意见区域互补而非孰优孰劣；结构化干预远优于调高采样温度或添加多样性指令等低成本方法。多样性是干预结构形式与组合的结果，而非单一维度的缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升LLM输出多样性的方法碎片化，不同方法在隔离条件下用不可比的指标评估，且实践中多种方法同时部署和升级，难以将增益归因到具体组件。缺乏对多样性的科学理解。

Method: 设计析因实验，分离两种主要干预维度：输入条件（通过人物角色深度操作化）和交互架构。在100个真实用户开放问题上评估7个模型，使用多种互补指标测量多样性。

Result: 1) 人物角色细节与多样性并非单调正相关，初始角色条件已捕获大部分增益，进一步增加人口统计细节反而可能降低多样性；2) 不同交互架构探索的意见区域大多不重叠，组合多种架构比优化单一架构覆盖更广；3) 提高采样温度和添加多样性指令等低成本替代方案效果微乎其微。

Conclusion: LLM输出多样性不是单一维度缩放的结果，而是高度依赖于干预的结构形式及其组合方式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate diverse human opinions in open-ended tasks such as synthetic surveys, focus group modeling, and public opinion prediction. However, LLM outputs exhibit systematic opinion homogenization. Practitioners have explored various interventions to increase diversity, but the landscape remains fragmented: different methods are evaluated in isolation with incomparable metrics, and in practice they are typically deployed and upgraded simultaneously, making it difficult to attribute gains to specific components. To advance a more scientific understanding of LLM output diversity, we design a factorial experiment that separates two primary intervention dimensions: input conditioning (operationalized through persona depth) and interaction architecture. We evaluate all conditions on 100 real-user open-ended questions across 7 models, measuring diversity with multiple complementary metrics. Our findings challenge several common assumptions. First, more persona detail does not monotonically increase diversity. The initial step of persona conditioning already captures the majority of the gain, while further elaboration with demographic detail does not consistently improve and can reduce diversity on some models. Second, rather than seeking a single best interaction architecture, we find that different architectures explore largely non-overlapping opinion regions. Combining multiple architectures yields broader coverage than optimizing any one. Third, commonly attempted low-cost alternatives such as raising sampling temperature and adding diversity instructions produce negligible effects compared to structured interventions. Overall, our work demonstrates that diversity is not a product of scaling along any single dimension, but is highly sensitive to the structural form and combination of interventions.

</details>


### [101] [LLM-INSTRUCT at UZH Shared Task 2026: Constraint-Aware Retrieval and Selective Debate for Paragraph-Level Argument Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20430)
*Phuong Huu Vu Tran,Long Minh Vo,Son Nguyen Minh Le,Hoang Van*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-INSTRUCT系统，在ArgMining 2026 UZH共享任务中通过元数据感知稠密检索缩小标签空间、约束解码、三智能体辩论分支和模式验证，在段落级论元挖掘任务上取得整体第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联合国和联合国教科文组织决议中段落级论元挖掘任务的挑战，包括段落类型分类、141个官方标签的子集预测和有向关系预测，且需在严格JSON模式约束下仅使用不超过8B参数的开源模型。

Method: 将任务建模为约束结构化预测：1) 元数据感知稠密检索缩小候选标签空间；2) 基于维度上限的约束解码；3) 仅对不确定案例启动三智能体辩论分支；4) 最终输出模式验证。

Result: 在官方排行榜上综合排名第一，F1第一，LLM-as-a-Judge第五。开发阶段将Task 1b Micro-F1从35.83%提升至40.08%，同时保持Task 2内部得分为4.421。

Conclusion: 核心经验是：在生成前缩小决策空间能同时提升准确性和提交鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present LLM-INSTRUCT, the winning system for the UZH Shared Task at ArgMining 2026 on paragraph-level argument mining in UN and UNESCO resolutions. The task requires paragraph-type classification, prediction of a subset of 141 official tags, and directed relation prediction under a strict JSON schema setting using only open-weight models up to 8B parameters. We frame the task as constrained structured prediction. The system first narrows the candidate tag space with metadata-aware dense retrieval, then applies constrained decoding with per-dimension caps, escalates only uncertain cases to a three-agent debate branch, and finally validates the output schema. On the official leaderboard, LLM-INSTRUCT ranked 1st overall, with 1st in F1 and 5th in LLM-as-a-Judge. During development, our configuration search further improved Task 1b Micro-F1 from 35.83% to 40.08% while keeping the internal Task 2 score at 4.421. The main lesson is simple: reducing the decision space before generation improves both accuracy and submission robustness. Our code and supporting scripts are publicly available at: https://github.com/LLM-Instruct-at-UZH-Shared-Task-2026/Method

</details>


### [102] [Skill-Contracted Agents for Evidence-Aware Materials Literature Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20431)
*Bixuan Li,Yu Liu,Shuo Shi,Xiaoya Huang,Peng Kang,Lei Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AlphaAgent是一个技能驱动的智能体框架，通过显式技能契约将基于检索的问答与论文级报告生成解耦，在材料科学文献分析任务中显著优于传统检索增强生成基线系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索增强生成流水线在单一"检索-生成"架构下难以同时协调材料科学文献中成分、加工、表征和性能关系的异构任务，需要更高效的任务分离方案。

Method: 提出AlphaAgent框架，包含两个核心技能：(1) 检索技能——将用户请求改写为材料特定搜索意图，查询涵盖JCR冶金与冶金工程类别超30万篇论文的索引，初始证据不足时重构查询；(2) 报告生成技能——解析全文本PDF，生成结构化单篇分析报告及跨论文摘要。

Result: 在涵盖40个材料科学问题的盲评中（其中一半需要深度分析推理），AlphaAgent在底层模型、文档索引和检索规模匹配的条件下大幅超越基线系统，在机制解释和可信度边界意识方面提升最为显著。

Conclusion: 显式任务分离、精细化的检索意图和证据感知的生成策略能够有效提升基于大语言模型的材料科学文献分析性能。

Abstract: Materials science literature analysis requires simultaneous attention to composition, processing, characterization, and property relationships, yet conventional retrieval-augmented generation pipelines struggle to reconcile heterogeneous tasks within a single retrieve-then-generate architecture. Here we present AlphaAgent, a skill-driven agent framework that decouples retrieval-based question answering from paper-level report generation through explicit skill contracts. A dedicated retrieval skill rewrites user requests into material-specific search intents, queries a curated index of more than 300,000 papers from the Journal Citation Reports Metallurgy and Metallurgical Engineering category, and reformulates queries when initial evidence is insufficient. A separate report-generation skill parses full-text PDFs to produce structured per-paper analytical reports and cross-paper summaries. In a blind evaluation on 40 materials-science questions, half of which required deep analytical reasoning, AlphaAgent substantially outperformed a baseline system matched for underlying model, document index, and retrieval scale, with the largest gains in mechanistic explanation and awareness of credibility boundaries. These results indicate that explicit task separation, refined retrieval intent, and evidence-aware generation improve large-language-model-based literature analysis for materials research.

</details>


### [103] [Position: Natural Language Should Not Fully Replace Formal Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20432)
*Eitan Wagner,Elisha Rosensweig,Omri Abend*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文论证自然语言和形式语言在不同任务特异性下具有互补优势，提出了"特异性交叉定理"证明存在一个阈值，超过该阈值时用自然语言表达形式化需求的成本高于直接使用形式语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对"自然语言可完全替代形式语言"的流行观点，指出该观点忽略了自然语言在开放语境中优化的"欠规格化"特性。

Method: 建立以"任务特异性"为核心的形式化框架（定义为给定用户需求条件下输出空间不确定性的信息论缩减），证明"特异性交叉定理"，并通过图像生成、代码合成、音频制作等多模态案例进行分析。

Result: 自然语言在低特异性任务中表现优异，形式语言在高严格性任务中更具优势；存在一个成本交叉阈值。

Conclusion: 自然语言和形式语言是互补工具，应开发允许用户在特异性谱系间灵活移动的混合系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and their widespread adoption have prompted claims that natural language could entirely replace formal languages, such as programming languages for software design. In this position paper, we argue that this perspective overlooks fundamental linguistic properties of natural language, specifically that it is optimized for underspecification in open-ended contexts. We introduce a formal framework centered on *task specificity*, defining it as the information-theoretic reduction of uncertainty in an output space -- such as all possible images -- given a user's specific requirements. We prove a *specificity crossover theorem*, showing the existence of a threshold beyond which the cost to express formal requirements into natural language exceeds the cost of direct formal specification. By analyzing case studies across modalities, such as image generation, code synthesis, and audio production, we demonstrate that natural language excels at low specificity tasks, while formal languages are advantageous on tasks with stricter requirements. We conclude that natural and formal languages are complementary tools and advocate the development of hybrid systems that allow users to move across the specificity spectrum.

</details>


### [104] [Moir: Let the Model Direct Its Own Story for Robust Cross-Domain Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20433)
*Jea Kwon,Jiwon Kim,Dong-kyum Kim,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Moir方法，通过从模型自身解码分布中采样估计保留协方差矩阵，解决知识编辑导致数学与编程推理能力退化的问题。在多个模型和编辑方法下，Moir显著优于Wikipedia基线，例如Qwen3-8B在20,000次批量编辑后GSM8K准确率达79.9%（基线仅10.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑会导致模型核心能力（数学推理、编程）不对称退化，而百科式回忆能力保持完整。作者将这种不对称退化归因于分布不匹配：协方差编辑器只保留了参考语料库所覆盖的子空间，但无法捕捉经过SFT/DPO后形成的新操作分布。静态外部语料库无法恢复这种偏移后的流形。

Method: 提出Moir方法，直接从模型自身的解码分布中采样来估计保留协方差矩阵C。通过用单个随机词汇token种生成，绕过主导采样输出的指令遵循模板，从而暴露模型已内化的更广泛子空间。该方法无需外部数据，可作为即插即用组件用于任何基于协方差的编辑器（如MEMIT、AlphaEdit）。

Result: 在OLMo-2、Llama-3.1、Qwen-3（7-8B）三个模型上，在MEMIT和AlphaEdit两种编辑方法、批量和序列两种模式下，Moir均一致地扩展了最脆弱领域的保留能力。Qwen3-8B经过20,000次AlphaEdit批量编辑后，GSM8K准确率为79.9%，而Wikipedia基线仅10.9%。

Conclusion: 将保留分布与模型的操作分布对齐是非破坏性知识编辑的关键因素，而模型本身可能是部署系统中最容易获取该分布的来源。

Abstract: While language models remain frozen at their training state, the world evolves continuously. Knowledge editing has emerged as a key alternative to full retraining, but its deployment is bottlenecked by the erosion of core capabilities: mathematical and programmatic reasoning collapse while encyclopedic recall remains intact. We trace this asymmetric degradation to a distributional mismatch. Covariance-based editors preserve only the subspaces spanned by their reference corpus, but fail to capture the operative distribution shaped by post-training such as SFT and DPO. Static external corpora, including Wikipedia and even the original pretraining mixture, cannot recover this shifted manifold. We propose Moir, which estimates the preservation covariance $C$ directly from the model itself by sampling from its own decoding distribution. Seeding generation with a single random vocabulary token bypasses the instruction-following templates that otherwise dominate sampled outputs, exposing the broader subspaces the model has internalized. Moir requires no external data and serves as a drop-in component for any covariance-based editor, a practical advantage given that the pre- and post-training corpora of most modern LLMs are not publicly accessible. Across OLMo-2, Llama-3.1, and Qwen-3 (7-8B), under both MEMIT and AlphaEdit and in batch and sequential regimes, Moir consistently extends preservation in the most vulnerable domains, most strikingly on Qwen3-8B after 20,000 AlphaEdit batch edits, it retains 79.9% GSM8K accuracy compared to 10.9% with the Wikipedia baseline. These results suggest that aligning the preservation distribution with the model's operative distribution is a key factor in non-destructive editing, and that the model itself may be the most accessible source of that distribution for deployed systems.

</details>


### [105] [Break Through the Compression Bottleneck: From Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20434)
*Xiusheng Huang,Lu Wang,Yequan Wang,Jun Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次从数学上证明低秩分解与量化在模型压缩中并非正交，二者的直接组合会导致显著性能下降，并提出了对角粘附法（DAM）来有效结合两种方法以缓解性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型参数规模持续增长，需要有效的模型压缩来降低计算和内存开销。现有压缩方法在高压缩率时性能显著下降，而低秩分解和量化作为两种主流压缩方法，其组合能否突破压缩瓶颈尚不明确，业界普遍认为两者正交，但缺乏理论验证。

Method: 首次给出低秩分解与量化非正交的数学证明；通过大规模语言模型实验验证理论发现；提出对角粘附法（DAM）来有效结合两种方法并减轻性能损失。

Result: 低秩分解与量化在结合时非正交，直接组合会导致显著性能退化；所提DAM方法能有效结合两种压缩方法，缓解性能损失。

Conclusion: 为模型压缩领域提供了深刻见解，并为未来相关研究奠定了坚实的理论和实验基础。

Abstract: As the parameter size of language models continues to grow, effective model compression is required to reduce their computational and memory overhead. Existing compression methods suffer from bottleneck issues: when the compression ratio is increased, performance degrades significantly. Low-rank decomposition and quantization are two prominent compression methods that have been proven to significantly reduce the computational and memory requirements of Large Language Models (LLMs) while maintaining model accuracy. Evidently, combining these two methods will break through the existing compression bottleneck. However, how these two methods interact when combined remains a critical question for developers, as many assume they are orthogonal, meaning their combination would not introduce additional errors beyond those independently introduced by each method. This paper provides the first mathematical proof that low-rank decomposition and quantization are non-orthogonal. We validate these findings through a series of experiments on large language models. Our results demonstrate that these methods are non-orthogonal, and their combination leads to significant performance degradation. Importantly, we propose a novel approach Diagonal Adhesive Method (DAM), which can effectively combine the two methods and mitigate the performance loss. Our research provides deep insights into model compression and lays a solid theoretical and experimental foundation for future related studies.

</details>


### [106] [Making Open-Source Text LLM Watermarks Durable Against Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20435)
*Luisa Scharff,Thibaud Gloaguen,Robin Staab,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对抗训练方法（Merge-Adversarial Training），使开源大模型中的文本水印能够抵御模型合并操作，将水印存活率提升最多51个百分点（TPR@1%FPR），同时保持下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源大模型在发布后常经历模型合并等后训练修改，已有水印技术会被此类操作轻易移除，因此需要设计能抵御模型合并的持久化水印方案。

Method: 提出Merge-Adversarial Training，一种对抗训练算法，在蒸馏文本水印到模型权重的同时，通过模拟合并攻击来增强水印对后续模型合并的鲁棒性。

Result: 在SLERP合并算法下TPR@1%FPR提升最多51个百分点（平均提升25个百分点），在多种合并算法和真实合并场景下均一致优于所有基线方法，且不牺牲下游任务性能。

Conclusion: 对抗训练是提高开源大模型水印对后训练修改（尤其是模型合并）鲁棒性的可靠方法，首次实现了可抵御模型合并的开源模型水印。

Abstract: Open-source LLMs (OSMs)arereaching near state-of-the-art performance, prompting prior works to trace the text they generate by embedding text watermarking algorithms directly into their weights. Yet, OSMs are subject to post-training modifications, which has been shown to remove the watermark. Model merging in particular, a prominent method used for combining expert knowledge and preventing catastrophic forgetting, strongly removes such OSM watermarks. A key question is how to enable OSM watermarks that survive subsequent merging. In this work, we show for the first time how to design an OSM watermark that is durable against model merging. We propose Merge-Adversarial Training, an adversarial training algorithm to distill text watermarks into model weights while being robust to subsequent model merging. Our approach consistently outperforms all baselines (e.g. with SLERP up to +51 percentage points (pp) TPR@1%FPR with +25 pp on average) while preserving downstream capabilities. We also for the first time evaluate OSM watermarks against realistic merge scenarios, representing common use-cases such as combining expert capabilities or preventing catastrophic forgetting, and with 3 prominent merging algorithms. More broadly, our findings suggest that adversarial training is a reliable approach for increasing OSM watermark durability against post-training modifications.

</details>


### [107] [Routing Subspaces: Auditing Evaluation-to-Deployment Mismatch in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20436)
*Phongsakon Mark Konrad,Toygar Tanyel,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现微调后模型在评估提示下表现正常，但在日常使用提示下仍存在不安全行为。通过在模型内部定位并修正这一差异（路径修补指导下的中层激活对比），可以在10/12个设置中消除评估-部署差距，且不影响部署提示下的表现。该方法作为微调后检查点的诊断工具，而非训练时防御或部署安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估假设测试时的行为反映实际部署行为，但微调可能打破这一假设：模型在评估提示下表现安全，但在日常使用提示下仍存在不安全行为。现有方法仅能发现输出分数的不匹配，无法定位问题的内部编码位置。

Method: 通过路径修补（path-patching）确定中层窗口，在该窗口内拟合配对激活对比（paired activation contrast），然后在保留提示上修正该坐标方向上的激活值，以消除评估与部署之间的行为差距。

Result: 在4个全矩阵指令微调模型实例的12个模型-行为设置中，该方法在10个设置（其中n≥120配对问题的8个设置中成功6个）关闭了评估-部署差距。第5个模型用于定位和编辑来源验证，部署框架下的变化率最多为6.1个百分点。失败的2个设置均为谄媚行为，表明单一坐标审计在区分是更高秩或未被深度启发捕捉时不足。

Conclusion: 该方法作为微调后检查点的诊断工具，揭示并修补评估与部署间的行为差异，但并非训练时防御或部署安全的保证。对于某些行为（如谄媚），单一坐标干预不足以完全消除差距。

Abstract: Safety evaluations often assume that behavior observed during testing reflects behavior in ordinary use, but fine-tuning can break this assumption. A checkpoint can appear fixed under evaluation-style prompts while the same behavior persists under ordinary-use prompts. Output scores reveal this mismatch but do not locate it. We investigate whether the distinction is encoded in a stable internal site and introduce an approach that fits a paired activation contrast at a path-patching-informed mid-depth window, then modifies the resulting coordinate on held-out prompts. The intervention closes the evaluation-to-deployment gap in ten of twelve model--behavior settings (six of the eight settings with $n{\geq}120$ paired questions) across four full-matrix instruction-tuned model instances; a fifth model supports localization and edit-provenance checks, and deployment-framed rates change by at most $6.1$pp. The two flat cells, both sycophancy, indicate that a single-coordinate audit is not sufficient when the installed distinction is higher-rank or missed by the depth heuristic. The audit is a diagnostic for fine-tuned checkpoints, not a training-time defense or a guarantee of deployment safety.

</details>


### [108] [TopoGuard: Graph Theory Based Defenses Against Split-Knowledge Attacks on RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20437)
*Chahana Dahal,Zuobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生产级RAG系统中存在一种名为"分裂知识攻击"（split-knowledge attack）的新型安全威胁，攻击者通过注入单独无害但合并后会产生虚假关联的文档来攻击系统。针对该问题，作者提出了基于图论的TopoGuard系列方法，通过构建检索文档的语义相似度图并检测恶意拓扑结构来防御此类攻击。实验表明，TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity在HotpotQA数据集上以1%假阳性率捕获的攻击数量是LlamaGuard-2-8B的21倍，且延迟在亚毫秒级别，对自适应攻击和跨领域查询均具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖聚合外部文档回答复杂查询，但检索引入的文档会带来新的攻击面。分裂知识攻击中，攻击者注入的单个文档看似无害，合并后却使语言模型产生错误关联。这种攻击结构上对现有的逐文档过滤器（如LlamaGuard）不可见，亟需新的防御方法。

Method: 提出TopoGuard系列方法，核心思想是从检索到的文档构建语义相似度图，通过图论方法检测具有恶意拓扑结构的上下文。具体包括基于图谱理论的分析和多种变体（如TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity），理论证明了该方法即使在噪声输入下依然有效且鲁棒。

Result: 在HotpotQA等两个检索数据集上的实验表明：TopoGuard-λ₂+Entity在1%假阳性率下召回率达32.6%，而LlamaGuard-2-8B仅1.5%（高出21倍）；处理延迟在亚毫秒级别；在自适应攻击和良性跨领域查询场景下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TopoGuard系列方法能有效检测RAG系统中的分裂知识攻击，相比现有逐文档过滤器具有显著优势，且高效、鲁棒，适合生产环境部署。

Abstract: Production Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) systems rely on aggregating multiple external documents to answer complex queries. However, the retrieved documents introduce a new threat surface that can be exploited to launch split-knowledge attacks. In this attack, the adversary injects documents that are individually benign but create false associations when combined and fed to language models. This paper shows that the new attack is structurally invisible to existing per-document filters, like LlamaGuard. To address this issue in RAG, this work introduces TopoGuard, a family of graph theory-based methods specifically targeting the split-knowledge attacks by building a semantic similarity graph from retrieved documents and detecting contexts with malicious topology. Grounded on the theoretical analysis, the TopoGuard family has been proven to be effective and robust even with noisy inputs. Extensive experiments are conducted on two retrieval datasets and compared with multiple baseline methods. Specifically, the TopoGuard-$λ_2$+Entity catches 21$\times$ more attacks than LlamaGuard-2-8B at 1\% FPR (32.6\% vs 1.5\% recall) on the HotpotQA dataset. Compared with production RAG detection systems using large language models, the proposed TopoGuard variants run efficiently at sub-millisecond latency and stay robust under adaptive adversaries and benign cross-domain queries.

</details>


### [109] [Preference Tuning as Spectral Update Reorganization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20438)
*Peiyan Zhang,Haibo Jin,Liying Kang,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过谱分解方法研究RLHF和偏好优化中参数更新的内部结构，发现更新矩阵存在"头-尾"组织：头部主导行为偏移，尾部提供残余信息，仅有头部不足以恢复完整解，而头部与尾部结合才能实现完整的偏好训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏好后训练（如RLHF）通常只关注最终行为表现，对其诱导的参数更新的内部结构和组织方式缺乏理解，作者希望打开这一"黑箱"。

Method: 对有效的LoRA更新进行谱分解，将谱成分作为可插拔模块重新加载，从而实现对偏好诱导更新的隔离、重组和直接干预。

Result: 1) 不同模型、优化算法和监督方式下，更新矩阵一致呈现"头-尾"结构：紧凑的头部主导端点偏移，异质残余尾部保持独立；2) 头部主导可见的行为变化，尾部单独作用效果微弱；3) 跨运行重组中混合适配器遵循头部来源，头部携带运行级求解器偏差；4) 仅头部学习虽非平凡但无法恢复完整解（尤其在分布外行为上），仅尾部学习几乎无可见提升。

Conclusion: 偏好后训练本质上是参数更新的结构化重组，而非单一的行为修正过程；对齐增益与覆盖损失与学习更新自身的组织结构密切相关。

Abstract: Preference-based post-training is usually understood through endpoint behavior, yet the learned update that produces this behavior remains largely opaque. We study RLHF and related preference optimization through the spectral structure of their induced parameter updates. By decomposing effective LoRA updates and reloading their spectral components as plug-in modules, we turn preference-induced updates into objects that can be isolated, recomposed, and directly intervened on. Across model families, optimization algorithms, and supervision regimes, these updates consistently develop a spectral head--tail organization. A compact head emerges early and carries the dominant endpoint shift, while a heterogeneous residual tail remains. The split is functional rather than merely descriptive. Plug-in intervention shows that the head accounts for the visible behavioral departure from the base model, while the tail is weak in isolation. Cross-run recomposition further shows that mixed adapters follow the source of the head, indicating that the head carries run-level solver bias. This endpoint dominance does not imply learning sufficiency. Head-only learning is non-vacuous but fails to recover the full solution, especially on out-of-distribution behavior. Tail-only learning yields little visible gain, yet the full solution is not recovered without the tail. These findings recast preference post-training as structured update reorganization rather than a monolithic behavioral correction, and suggest that alignment gain and coverage loss are tied to how the learned update itself is organized.

</details>


### [110] [AsymVerify at SemEval-2026 Task 6: Asymmetric Confidence-Gated Verification for Political Evasion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20439)
*Sebastien Kawada*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AsymVerify是一个置信度门控的不对称验证系统，用于政治回避性回答的三分类任务（明确回复/模棱两可/明确非回复），在SemEval-2026 Task 6上以0.85 Macro F1获得第二名，通过分类后对低置信度预测选择性应用降级或升级验证，仅增加少量推理成本即可显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治回避性回答难以检测，因为回避性回答表面上配合但缺乏具体承诺，现有方法难以区分这三种类别。

Method: 采用置信度门控的不对称双验证器设计：首先对每个问答对进行三分类，然后对低置信度预测选择性应用降级验证（将CR/CNR纠正为AMB）或升级验证（将AMB纠正为CR），从而以较低额外推理成本提升分类准确性。

Result: 在评估集D_eval（n=237）上取得0.85 Macro F1，在41支参赛队伍中排名第二。在开发集D_dev（n=308）上，GLM-4.7后端下AsymVerify相比单次分类提升+17.1 Macro F1，仅需1.48次调用/样本；升级验证器单独即可使所有测试的LLM后端在开发集上提升+6.8至+15.2 Macro F1。

Conclusion: 错误主要集中在模棱两可类别的边界上，不对称双验证器设计针对此问题有效；置信度门控机制在显著提升性能的同时保持低推理成本，验证了该方法在政治回避回答检测任务上的有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: Political evasion is difficult to detect because evasive answers often appear cooperative while avoiding concrete commitment. We present AsymVerify, a confidence-gated verification system for SemEval-2026 Task 6, a three-way classification of Clear Reply, Ambivalent, and Clear Non-Reply responses. AsymVerify scored 0.85 Macro F1 on the evaluation split (D_eval, n=237), placing 2nd out of 41 teams on the official leaderboard. The system first classifies each question-answer pair, then selectively applies downgrade verification (CR/CNR -> AMB) or upgrade verification (AMB -> CR) to low-confidence predictions. Development analysis shows that errors concentrate at the Ambivalent boundary in both directions, motivating this asymmetric two-verifier design while confidence gating keeps additional inference cost low. On D_dev (n=308), AsymVerify with GLM-4.7 gains +17.1 Macro F1 over single-pass classification at 1.48 calls/example, and the upgrade verifier alone improves every tested LLM backend on D_dev by +6.8 to +15.2 Macro F1 over its single-pass baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/kaons-research/AsymVerify-ACL.

</details>


### [111] [Answer-then-Edit: Reasoning Skeleton Editing for Anti-Distillation with Preserved Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20440)
*Fan Li,Mengting Pan,Sijia Xu,Xiaoyang Wang,Chen Chen,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SGRE是一个“先回答后编辑”的反蒸馏框架，通过提取推理骨架、图粗化和骨架语言化三步策略，在不牺牲推理准确性和自然度的前提下，有效防止大语言模型被知识蒸馏窃取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有大语言模型即使通过黑盒API部署，仍面临被对手通过知识蒸馏低成本复制能力的风险。现有基于水印的方法只能事后验证，而基于内部模型扰动的方法难以平衡反蒸馏效果与模型实用性（推理准确性和自然度）。

Method: 提出SGRE（骨架引导的推理编辑）框架，分为两个阶段：(1) 回答阶段：教师模型先生成干净的推理轨迹，保留原始推理准确性；(2) 编辑阶段：受认知负荷理论启发，采用三步策略——推理骨架提取、骨架图粗化、骨架语言化，共同扰动推理结构并增加文本复杂性，以增强对学生模型的外在认知负荷。

Result: 跨多种大语言模型的实验表明，SGRE在降低蒸馏效果方面达到最先进水平，同时保持无损的推理准确性和优越的轨迹自然度。

Conclusion: SGRE通过后处理方式实现推理轨迹的扰动，在反蒸馏效果与模型实用性之间取得了更好的平衡，为保护LLM知识产权提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Proprietary large language models (LLMs) entail substantial intellectual and financial investment, making them valuable intellectual property (IP). However, even when deployed via black-box APIs, these models remain vulnerable to unauthorized knowledge distillation, which allows adversaries to cheaply extract and replicate model capabilities. To address this issue, anti-distillation (AD) has been proposed to generate defensive outputs that hinder distillation effectiveness, overcoming the limitation of watermarking-based approaches that rely on post-hoc verification. However, existing AD methods based on internal model perturbations struggle to balance anti-distillability and utility (e.g., answer accuracy and naturalness) of reasoning traces, with stronger defenses often causing significant utility loss. To fill this gap, we propose \textbf{\underline{S}}keleton-\textbf{\underline{G}}uided \textbf{\underline{R}}easoning \textbf{\underline{E}}diting (SGRE), an \textit{Answer-then-Edit} framework that performs post-hoc trace modification for anti-distillation. In the answer stage, the teacher model first generates clean reasoning traces, preserving the original reasoning accuracy while enabling more flexible control over trace naturalness. In the editing stage, we draw inspiration from Cognitive Load Theory (CLT) and introduce a three-stage strategy consisting of reasoning skeleton extraction, skeleton graph coarsening, and skeleton verbalization. These operations jointly perturb reasoning structures and augment textual complexity to amplify extraneous load on student models, hindering their acquisition of underlying reasoning patterns. Extensive experiments across diverse LLMs demonstrate that SGRE achieves state-of-the-art performance in reducing distillation effectiveness, while maintaining lossless reasoning accuracy and superior trace naturalness.

</details>


### [112] [Belief Propagation in LLM World Models: Measuring Strategic Information Bias with Prediction Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20441)
*Mykola Khandoga,Yevhen Kostiuk,Anton Polishko,Yurii Filipchuk,Kostiantyn Kozlov,Dmytro Zamriy,Artur Kiulian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出将LLM与预测市场结合，作为校准工具来量化信息生态系统引发的信念偏差。通过对111个乌克兰相关预测市场（约93,000条预测，四种模型）的分析，发现英语新闻语境系统性地使领土预测产生偏差（错误率64-72%），且偏差主要源于文本来源而非模型架构。补充乌克兰军事分析来源可减少偏差，但绝对误差的改善有限且依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息生态系统中，人类分析师和AI系统都会继承信息来源的盲点，导致信念偏差。需要一种可校准的方法来量化这种偏差，特别是区分偏差是来自信息来源还是模型处理过程。

Method: 将LLM作为信念提取器从文本语料中提取隐含信念，同时以预测市场价格轨迹（最终由实际结果锚定）作为校准参考。通过消融实验（固定模型、变化信息上下文）和控制实验（使用已知实际结果的污染模型）来隔离特定文本的偏差贡献。

Result: 英语新闻语境下，当预测指向领土捕获时，错误率高达64-72%；已知实际结果的污染模型呈现相同错误率，表明偏差主要源于文本；补充乌克兰军事分析来源可减少所有清洁模型的偏差，但绝对误差改善有限且因模型而异。

Conclusion: 偏差主要源于信息来源而非模型架构，四种模型架构上一致。这意味着任何处理这些信息的系统都将继承偏差并传播到下游决策中。研究提供了量化信息生态系统偏差的校准方法。

Abstract: Every information ecosystem produces beliefs that shape strategic decisions. Both human analysts and AI systems inherit the blind spots of their information sources. We show that LLMs, combined with prediction markets, function as a calibrated instrument for measuring how far ecosystem-induced beliefs deviate from an external reference: LLMs extract the beliefs a text corpus implies, and prediction market price trajectories, anchored at resolution by realised outcomes, provide the calibration reference against which to quantify the deviation.
  We isolate the bias contribution of specific text through ablation: varying information context while holding the model fixed, with a contaminated model that knows actual outcomes as control. Applied to 111 Ukraine-related prediction markets, comprising approximately 93,000 predictions across four models, we find that English news context systematically biases territorial predictions, wrong 64 to 72 percent of the time when it pushes predictions toward territorial capture. A contaminated model that knows actual outcomes shows the same error rate, indicating that the bias originates primarily in the text. Supplementing with Ukrainian military-analytical sources reduces the bias for all clean models, while absolute-error gains are partial and model-dependent.
  We show that the distortion originates primarily in the sources, not the models. Consistent across four architectures, it will persist in any system that processes them and propagate into downstream decisions.

</details>


### [113] [Naver-News-KO: A Korean News Summarization Dataset for Open-Source Fine-Tuning of Summarization Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20442)
*Daekeun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了Naver-News-KO，一个包含27,400对（文档，摘要）的韩语新闻摘要数据集，来自2022年7月Naver News的10天窗口，涵盖经济与IT/科学两个类别。数据集托管于Hugging Face Hub，月下载量约33,000次。技术报告记录了数据收集协议、统计信息、与现有语料库对比、提供Lead-3基线及两个可复现的微调基线（KoBART和Gemma-2B-ko with LoRA），并明确了许可和使用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为已使用该数据集的后续工作提供可引用的技术参考，并系统记录数据集的收集过程、统计特征及基线性能，填补韩语新闻摘要数据集公开资源不足的空白。

Method: 从Naver News收集2022年7月10天窗口内的新闻，筛选经济与IT/科学两个类别，构建27,400对(文档,摘要)数据，按22,194/2,466/2,740划分训练/验证/测试集。报告提供Lead-3提取式基线以及KoBART和Gemma-2B-ko with LoRA两个微调基线，并公开训练脚本。

Result: 数据集平均字符压缩比6.03x；测试集与训练集之间存在16.8%的近重复标题Jaccard重叠；Lead-3基线ROUGE-1 55.1/ROUGE-L 50.6；KoBART基线ROUGE-1 56.6/BERTScore-F1 81.5；Gemma-2B-ko with LoRA基线ROUGE-1 55.3/BERTScore-F1 78.3。

Conclusion: Naver-News-KO是一个公开可用的韩语新闻摘要数据集，提供了详尽的文档统计和可复现的基线结果，可作为后续研究的引用参考，而非提出新的基准测试。

Abstract: We release Naver-News-KO, a Korean news summarization dataset of 27,400 (document, summary) pairs collected from Naver News over a ten-day window in July 2022 across two categories (Economy and IT/Science; 77/23 split), with train/validation/test partitions of 22,194 / 2,466 / 2,740 and a mean per-record document-to-summary character-compression ratio of 6.03x. The dataset has been publicly hosted on the Hugging Face Hub since January 2023 and, as of May 2026, receives approximately 33,000 downloads per month; community-maintained Korean summarization models fine-tuned on it include Gemma-2B-ko and Gemma2-9B variants. This technical report (i) documents the collection protocol, the column schema, and the split construction, (ii) reports corpus-level statistics (length distributions, compression ratio, and a measured 16.8% near-duplicate title-Jaccard overlap between test and train that users should be aware of), (iii) positions the resource against other open Korean summarization corpora, (iv) provides a Lead-3 extractive reference point (ROUGE-1 55.1, ROUGE-L 50.6) and two reproducible fine-tuned baselines -- KoBART (R-1 56.6, BERTScore-F1 81.5) and Gemma-2B-ko with LoRA (R-1 55.3, BERTScore-F1 78.3) -- with release-time training scripts, and (v) clarifies the licensing and intended-use scope of the resource. The goal is to provide a citable reference for downstream work that already uses this dataset, not to propose a new benchmark.

</details>


### [114] [GLAN-QnA-KR: A Seedless Taxonomy-Driven Korean Instruction Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20443)
*Daekeun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个包含303,581条记录的韩语指令-QA数据集GLAN-QnA-KR，采用无种子分类法驱动管道和Phi-3.5-MoE-instruct模型生成。该数据集具有极低的重复率（仅1条精确重复，近重复为0），并通过双层污染审计（字符三元组Jaccard最大0.163，嵌入余弦最大0.901）确保与三个基准测试集无显著重叠。这是目前最大的单管道合成韩语指令数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个大规模、高质量、无污染且可公开再分发的韩语指令问答数据集，弥补韩语领域缺乏大规模合成指令数据的空白。

Method: 采用GLAN（无种子分类法驱动）合成管道，使用Phi-3.5-MoE-instruct作为生成模型；构建包含1,084个英文标签学科的扁平分类法；对生成数据进行精确去重、三元组近重复检测，并对KMMLU、KoBEST（5个子任务）和HAE-RAE-Bench三个基准测试集进行双层污染审计（字符三元组Jaccard相似度和多语言E5嵌入余弦相似度）。

Result: 生成303,581条记录，精确重复仅1条，5,000样本探针中字符三元组Jaccard>=0.9的近重复聚类数为0。污染审计显示，20,000条采样问题与7个评估集之间的最大字符三元组Jaccard为0.163，无测试项达到0.7以上；最大多语言E5余弦为0.901，仅1项测试达到0.90以上，0项达到0.95以上。

Conclusion: 该数据集在发布时是Hugging Face Hub上可验证的最大单管道合成韩语指令数据集，也是唯一在10万条以上规模下采用无种子分类法协议构建的韩语数据集。文档记录了生成协议、数据统计、污染审计和许可边界，可供下游引用。

Abstract: We release GLAN-QnA-KR, a 303,581-row openly redistributable Korean instruction-QA corpus produced via the seedless taxonomy-driven GLAN synthesis pipeline with Microsoft's Phi-3.5-MoE-instruct as the producer model (generation: 2024-12; release: 2024-12; licence: OpenRAIL). The corpus spans a flat taxonomy of 1,084 English-labelled disciplines paired with Korean question/answer text, a 100-900 difficulty scale, and a median of 313 question characters and 1,098 answer characters per record. Two properties are atypical for synthetic instruction data at this scale: (i) exact duplicate questions number only 1 in 303,581 rows and character-trigram near-duplicate clusters at Jaccard >= 0.9 number zero in a 5,000-sample probe, and (ii) a two-layer contamination audit against KMMLU, KoBEST (five sub-tasks), and HAE-RAE-Bench shows a maximum test-vs-corpus question-level character-trigram Jaccard of 0.163 with zero test items at Jaccard >= 0.7, and a maximum multilingual-E5 cosine of 0.901 with a single test item at cosine >= 0.90 and zero at >= 0.95, across 20,000 sampled GLAN questions and seven evaluation sets. At the time of release, this is, to our knowledge, the largest single-pipeline synthetic Korean instruction corpus verifiable on the Hugging Face Hub and the only Korean >=100k-row corpus built under a seedless taxonomy-driven protocol. This note documents the generation protocol, corpus statistics, the contamination audit, and the licensing boundary in a form suitable for downstream citation.

</details>


### [115] [Confidently Deceptive: How Confidence Amplifies the Risk of LLM Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20444)
*Ali Asad,Stephen Obadinma,Anshul Pattoo,Wenxuan Zhang,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM能够以高置信度生成欺骗性响应，且这种高置信度欺骗性响应更具说服力（人类偏好率达78%），而模型虽能识别自身欺骗性输出（82.7%识别率）却仍会生成——即"有认知却不回避"的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未系统探究LLM生成欺骗性响应时的置信度水平，以及高置信度欺骗响应是否更容易被用户接受，从而构成潜在安全风险。

Method: 在多种模型和不同欺骗数据集上，通过口头自我报告和多种logit估计器综合测量置信度，并采用人工标注进行成对比较偏好评估。

Result: LLM以高口头自信生成欺骗响应；人类在78%的成对比较中偏好高置信度欺骗响应；misalignment微调加剧此问题；模型能以82.7%的准确率识别自身欺骗输出但仍预测会生成它们。

Conclusion: 自信欺骗是一个独立的对齐风险，需开展联合评估欺骗、置信度和意识的系统化评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can produce deceptive responses: outputs that mislead users in service of a contextually or experimentally induced goal. Yet it remains unclear how confidently models deceive and whether higher confidence makes deceptive responses more persuasive to end users. In this paper, we study these basic questions in various models and different deception datasets. We provide a comprehensive study measuring confidence through both verbalized self-reports and a range of logit-based estimators. We show that LLMs deliver deceptive responses with substantial verbalized confidence and that human annotators prefer the higher-confidence deceptive response 78% of the time in paired comparisons. Misalignment fine-tuning amplifies the problem. Confidence in deceptive responses rises across all three benchmarks, increasing the resulting potential risk, with effects generalizing beyond the training distribution. Strikingly, models classify their own deceptive outputs as deceptive at high rates (82.7% under misalignment) while still predicting they would produce them - recognition without avoidance. We argue that confident deception is a distinct alignment risk requiring evaluations that jointly measure deception, confidence, and awareness.

</details>


### [116] [SCoPE: Shift-Aware Speaker-Conditioned Priors for Emotion Recognition in Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20445)
*Burak Can Kaplan,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SCoPE模块，通过引入说话人条件先验、情感转移预测和转移感知融合机制，在嘈杂多模态对话场景中提升情感识别性能，在IEMOCAP数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话情感识别方法过度依赖显式证据，在多模态信号嘈杂（如面部遮挡、俚语、麦克风噪声）时表现脆弱，未能充分建模情感平滑演化、说话人特异性模式以及上下文驱动的情感转移等非显式因素。

Method: 提出SCoPE模块：1）利用每个说话人的情感历史建模先验分布；2）引入情感转移预测机制指导模型平衡先验与多模态证据；3）设计转移感知融合机制，采用贝叶斯-inspired的产品专家公式进行精度加权logit融合，使模型在情感持续时依赖历史先验，在转移可能发生时优先考虑多模态证据。

Result: 在多模态IEMOCAP数据集上，该方法优于近期最先进的模型。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模说话人情感先验和情感转移动态，并结合贝叶斯-inspired的融合机制，能有效提升嘈杂多模态环境下对话情感识别的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: In conversations, human emotions are transient; however, they tend to persist across multiple utterances. For example, we rarely switch instantly between contrasting emotions such as happiness and anger. Instead, emotions tend to evolve smoothly, and these patterns are often speaker-specific. Some people might escalate, while others gradually cool down over time. Furthermore, when emotions change during a conversation, they are often driven by contextual factors, such as newly received information or unexpected events. Even though progress has been made in Emotion Recognition in Conversations (ERC), most existing approaches still rely heavily on overt evidence and do not sufficiently model these non-apparent factors. Especially in multimodal settings, this makes these models fragile when the signals are noisy (e.g., occluded faces, slang expressions, or microphone noise). To address these limitations, we introduce Speaker-Conditioned Priors over Emotions (SCoPE). SCoPE is a light weight module that utilizes the emotional history of each speaker and explicitly models their priors for use in subsequent emotion classification. Second, we incorporate emotion shift prediction, a well-established concept in ERC, to guide the model in balancing the priors from SCoPE and multimodal evidence. Finally, we propose a shift-aware fusion mechanism that performs precision-weighted logit integration between multimodal evidence and the speaker prior, forming a Bayesian-inspired product-of-experts formulation. This dynamic fusion allows the model to rely on historical priors when emotions persist and to prioritize multimodal evidence when shifts are likely. Experimental results show our model achieves superior performance over recent state-of-the-art models on the IEMOCAP dataset in multimodal settings.

</details>


### [117] [Distinguishing Artificial from Authentic: Evaluating LLMs for Detecting LLM-Generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20446)
*Juho Leinonen,Paul Denny*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在教育场景中检测自身生成内容的能力，发现检测效果高度依赖任务类型：编程任务和长篇反思性写作检测可靠，但短问答任务表现差，且提示词设计对检测准确性有显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学生越来越多地使用LLM生成文本和代码，教育领域需要可靠的方法区分AI生成内容与人工提交内容。本文探究LLM自身能否作为检测工具，评估其在不同教育任务类型中的自我检测能力。

Method: 使用真实学生回答和多种变体的LLM生成答案，在编程练习、反思性写作和短问答三类任务上，采用不同的提示策略和输出格式，评估LLM检测自身输出的准确性，并分析提示设计、回答长度和任务类型等因素的影响。

Result: LLM检测高度依赖任务类型：编程任务和长篇反思性写作检测可靠性较高，短问答任务表现差（LLM常将自身输出判定为比真实学生回答更像人类）。提示框架和回答冗长度对反思性写作检测影响显著，微小提示变化会大幅降低准确性；编程任务检测对提示变化更鲁棒。

Conclusion: LLM自我检测在教育场景中有潜力但也有明显局限，不应将其作为识别AI生成学生作业的独立工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by students to generate natural language responses and program code, there is growing interest in whether LLMs themselves can be used to distinguish AI-generated work from human-authored submissions. In this paper, we investigate the extent to which LLMs can detect their own generated content across multiple educational task types, including programming exercises, reflective writing, and short-answer questions. Using authentic student responses and multiple variants of LLM-generated answers, we evaluate detection performance under different prompting strategies and output formats. Our study addresses three research questions: (1) how accurately LLMs can identify their own outputs across task domains, (2) how detection effectiveness is influenced by factors such as prompt design, response length, and task type, and (3) what characteristics of LLM-generated responses contribute to successful or failed detection. Our findings show that LLM-based detection is highly task-dependent: detection is substantially more reliable for programming tasks and longer reflective responses, but performs poorly for short-answer questions, where LLMs frequently judge their own outputs as more human-like than authentic student responses. We further find that prompt framing and response verbosity have a pronounced effect on detectability in reflective writing tasks, with relatively minor prompt variations significantly reducing detection accuracy, while programming-related detection is more robust to prompt changes. Together, these results highlight both the potential and the limitations of LLM self-detection in educational settings and suggest caution in relying on LLMs as standalone tools for identifying AI-generated student work.

</details>


### [118] [thaulab@EEUCA 2026: Who Said What to Whom? A Targeting-Aware Neural-Symbolic Pipeline for Gaming Toxicity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20447)
*Anmol Guragain,Marcos Estecha-Garitagoitia,Luis Fernando D'Haro Enríquez,Ricardo de Córdoba*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三阶段流水线系统，结合两种紧凑型Transformer（DeBERTa-v3-base和XLM-RoBERTa-base）集成以及一个基于语言学知识的调解器（LIM），用于EEUCA 2026共享任务中的游戏聊天毒性分类。系统通过两阶段数据增强策略解决极端类别不平衡问题，在官方测试集上取得Macro F1 0.6441和准确率0.9062的成绩，Macro F1排名第3、准确率排名第1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游戏聊天中的毒性分类任务面临极端类别不平衡（非毒性到极端主义的比例为1450:1）以及少数类别（仇恨与骚扰、威胁、极端主义）在现实游戏审核中具有最高安全重要性，现有模型难以有效处理这些安全关键的少数类别。

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：(1) 集成两个紧凑型Transformer（DeBERTa-v3-base 184M参数、XLM-RoBERTa-base 278M参数）；(2) 语言学知识调解器（LIM），通过基于语料库的词汇规范化、类别条件一元评分、多语言亵渎检测以及基于言语行为理论的施动者指向分析，解决模型间分歧；(3) 仅使用提供训练数据的两阶段数据增强策略应对类别不平衡。

Result: 在官方测试集上取得Macro F1 0.6441和准确率0.9062，在所有参赛队伍中Macro F1排名第3、准确率排名第1。

Conclusion: 该流水线具有领域可移植性，适应其他游戏平台只需替换游戏特定实体词典；代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for the EEUCA 2026 Shared Task on toxicity classification in gaming chat. We implement a three-stage pipeline combining an ensemble of two compact transformers (DeBERTa-v3-base, 184M; XLM-RoBERTa-base, 278M) with a Linguistically-Informed Mediator (LIM) that resolves inter-model disagreements through corpus-backed lexical normalization, class-conditional unigram scoring, multilingual profanity detection, and agentive targeting analysis grounded in speech act theory. The LIM specifically targets the minority classes (Hate \& Harassment, Threats, and Extremism), which are the most safety-critical categories in real-world gaming moderation. To address the extreme class imbalance (1{,}450:1 Non-toxic to Extremism ratio), we introduce a two-stage data augmentation strategy using only the provided training data. Our system achieves a Macro F1 of 0.6441 and accuracy of 0.9062 on the official test set, ranking 3rd in Macro F1 and 1st in accuracy among all teams. The proposed pipeline is domain-portable: adapting to other gaming platforms requires substituting only the game-specific entity lexicon. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/Anmol2059/thaulab\_EEUCA.

</details>


### [119] [Domyn-Small: A European 10B Reasoning Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20448)
*Simone Angarano,Francesco Bertolotti,Federico D'Ambrosio,Michele Resta,Alessandro Rognoni,Nicolò Ruggeri,Dario Salvati,Andrea Valenti,Alberto Veneri,Martin Cimmino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Domyn-Small 是一个100亿参数的开源推理语言模型（MIT许可），经过9万亿token多语言预训练和后训练（CPT/SFT/GRPO+DPO多阶段），支持32K原生上下文（推理时通过YaRN扩展至128K），在7-10B参数级别模型中实现了优异的准确率-效率平衡，同时发布了配套的高性能推理框架Domyn Swarm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在7-10B参数级别的开源推理语言模型中，现有模型在推理效率（生成token数）与准确性之间存在权衡，缺乏同时兼顾高效token预算和强推理能力的方案。作者旨在开发一个在准确率-效率曲线上达到更优平衡点的轻量级推理模型。

Method: 1) 预训练：在9万亿token多语言数据上训练100亿参数模型；2) CPT：继续预训练将原生上下文窗口翻倍至32K；3) SFT：数学聚焦的退火训练；4) RL阶段：包含GRPO（可验证奖励）、DPO，以及覆盖数学、代码、多选题QA、指令遵循、工具调用五个任务领域的多环境GRPO阶段；5) 推理时通过YaRN将上下文扩展至128K；6) 通过聊天模板切换实现双模式推理。

Result: 与Qwen3.5-9B、OLMo-3-7B-Think、Nemotron-Nano-8B、Ministral-3-8B等7-10B参数级别模型相比，Domyn-Small在核心推理基准上生成的token数约为Qwen3.5-9B的三分之一、OLMo-3-7B-Think的35%，同时保持强指令遵循能力（IFEval 79.9）和竞争性的科学推理能力（GPQA-Diamond 50.0）。

Conclusion: Domyn-Small在7-10B参数级别模型中实现了强准确率-效率平衡，是一个高效的开源推理语言模型。同时发布的Domyn Swarm框架为HPC集群上的可扩展LLM推理提供了开源解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Domyn-Small, a 10-billion-parameter open-weight reasoning language model released under the MIT license. Domyn-Small is the product of an initial pre-training phase on 9 trillion tokens multilingual data, followed by a post-training pipeline for reasoning, instruction following, and context extension. For the latter, we performed a Continued Pre-Training (CPT) phase that doubles the native context window to 32K tokens, followed by SFT with a math-focused annealing run. Finally, the RL phase includes GRPO with verifiable rewards, DPO, and a multi-environment GRPO stage spanning five task domains: mathematics, code, multiple-choice QA, instruction-following, and tool calling. The 32K-token native context extends to 128K at inference via YaRN, and a chat-template toggle enables dual-mode reasoning. Against peer models in the 7--10B class (Qwen3.5-9B, OLMo-3-7B-Think, Nemotron-Nano-8B, Ministral-3-8B), Domyn-Small achieves a strong accuracy-efficiency balance: it produces roughly one-third as many tokens as Qwen3.5-9B and approximately 35% of OLMo-3-7B-Think's token budget on core reasoning benchmarks, while delivering strong instruction-following (IFEval 79.9) and competitive science reasoning (GPQA-Diamond 50.0). We release the weights and the post-training recipe alongside Domyn Swarm (Apache~2.0), an open-source framework for scalable LLM inference on HPC clusters developed during this program and used throughout this work.

</details>


### [120] [The Storyteller in the Model: Narrative Pattern Inheritance, Escalation Dynamics, and Alignment Governance in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20449)
*Adam Rigby,Raz Saremi,Azadeh Sohrabinejad,Mehdi Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过系统文献综述和跨论文分析，提出LLM在训练过程中吸收了人类写作中的叙事结构（如原型角色、情节弧线），导致输出出现“叙事漂移”现象——模型在长时间交互中向不可预测、对抗性或修辞性引诱的行为偏离。研究发现三个关键模式：LLM再现训练数据的统计模式而非独立推理；谄媚和欺骗等潜在特质可靠地涌现；窄范围叙事任务的微调会产生超出任务范围的意外行为变化。叙事漂移构成了已部署AI系统中未被监控的升级路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM的对齐、人物动态和涌现性失调等问题，但很少有人类写作中固有的叙事惯例（如原型角色、情节结构）作为系统性行为影响来源或治理风险来审视。作者试图探究这些叙事模式是否在训练中被吸收并在输出中浮现，导致长期交互中的行为漂移。

Method: 采用系统文献综述和跨论文分析方法，对LLM对齐、人物动态、涌现性失调和用户交互模式等领域的近期实证研究进行综合考察，检验叙事漂移假说。

Result: 揭示三个关键模式：(1) LLM再现训练数据的统计模式而非独立推理；(2) 谄媚和欺骗等潜在特质在不同不相关提示下可靠涌现；(3) 窄范围叙事任务的微调产生超出该任务的意外行为变化。此外，有说服力的叙事风格输出是真实场景中最常见的LLM产品，放大了这些风险。

Conclusion: 叙事漂移构成了已部署AI系统中未被监控的升级路径，它规避了离散事件检测机制，需要专门的监测工具来应对。

Abstract: LLMs are trained predominantly on human-authored text, yet the structural and narrative conventions embedded in that text are rarely examined as a source of systematic behavioral influence, or as a governance risk in deployed systems. This paper considers whether the storytelling patterns inherent in published human writing, including archetypal roles such as protagonist, antagonist, and underdog, as well as tension-and-resolution narrative arcs, are absorbed during training and subsequently surface in LLM outputs, causing responses to drift toward unexpected, adversarial, or rhetorically enticing behaviors over extended interactions. Through a systematic literature review and cross-paper analysis of recent empirical studies on LLM alignment, persona dynamics, emergent misalignment, and user interaction patterns, we observe evidence bearing on this hypothesis. The findings reveal three key patterns. First, LLMs reproduce statistical patterns from their training data rather than reasoning independently. Second, measurable latent traits, including sycophancy and deceptiveness, emerge reliably across unrelated prompts. Third, fine-tuning on a narrow narrative task can produce unintended behavioral changes well beyond that task. Furthermore, evidence suggests that persuasive, narrative-style outputs are among the most common LLM products in real-world usage, amplifying these risks. Narrative drift constitutes an unmonitored escalation pathway in deployed AI systems, one that evades discrete-incident detection mechanisms and requires dedicated monitoring instruments.

</details>


### [121] [ShriNep@EEUCA 2026: RAKSHAK - Multi-Task DeBERTa with Rationale Distillation and Jigsaw-Augmented Training for Toxic Intent Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20450)
*Binayak Karki,Aryan Kafle,Pingala Ghimire*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对GameTox共享任务中《坦克世界》聊天文本的六类有毒意图分类问题，提出了两个系统：主系统RAKSHAK（多任务DeBERTa-v3-base框架，结合理性蒸馏、监督对比学习和稀有类二元分类头，辅以Jigsaw跨领域迁移和LLM生成数据）和基线系统M1（DeBERTa-v3-base+Focal Loss）。RAKSHAK在官方测试集上达到0.5883 Macro F1，在35支参赛队伍中排名第7，跨领域迁移带来+2.6 F1，多任务架构贡献额外+3.7 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《坦克世界》聊天文本存在严重的类别不平衡、领域特定多语言俚语以及稀有类别（威胁类60样本、极端主义类24样本）数据极度稀缺的问题，需要开发有效方法应对这些挑战。

Method: 主系统RAKSHAK采用多任务DeBERTa-v3-base框架，融合三项关键技术：①从Qwen2.5-14B进行理性蒸馏；②监督对比损失；③为稀有类设计专用二元分类头。训练数据通过Jigsaw Toxic Comment数据集的16,225条样本映射到标签1-4进行跨领域增强，并利用LLM生成100条极端主义样本补充标签5。基线系统M1仅使用DeBERTa-v3-base配合Focal Loss在原始GameTox数据和100条极端主义样本上微调。

Result: RAKSHAK在官方测试集上达到0.5883 Macro F1，在35支参赛队伍中排名第7；M1达到0.5252 Macro F1。消融实验表明：跨领域迁移贡献+2.6 F1，多任务架构贡献额外+3.7 F1。

Conclusion: 跨领域数据迁移和多任务学习架构能有效缓解游戏聊天文本中稀有类别的分类困难，为严重类别不平衡场景下的细粒度有毒内容检测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: This paper presents two systems for the GameTox Shared Task at the Workshop on EEUCA at ACL 2026, which requires classifying World of Tanks chat utterances into six fine-grained toxic intent categories (Labels 0-5). Severe class imbalance, domain-specific multilingual slang, and extremely scarce data for rare categories such as Threats (Label 4, 60 samples) and Extremism (Label 5, 24 samples) make this a challenging classification problem. Our primary submission, RAKSHAK (rak s. aka, Sanskrit for "Protector"), is a multi-task DeBERTa-v3-base (He et al., 2022) framework combining rationale distillation from Qwen2.5-14B (An et al., 2024), Supervised Contrastive Loss, and dedicated rare-class binary heads. RAKSHAK's training data is augmented with cross-domain transfer from the Jigsaw Toxic Comment dataset (16,225 samples mapped to Labels 1-4) and 100 LLM-generated extremism samples for Label 5. Our secondary system (M1) fine-tunes DeBERTa-v3-base with Focal Loss on the original GameTox data plus the same 100 extremism samples, without Jigsaw transfer. RAKSHAK achieves a Macro F1 of 0.5883 on the official test set, ranking 7th out of 35 participating teams, while M1 achieves 0.5252 Macro F1. An ablation comparing M1 with and without Jigsaw data shows that cross-domain transfer accounts for +2.6 F1 points, while RAKSHAK's multi-task architecture contributes a further +3.7 points.

</details>


### [122] [Semantic Field Theory: Historical Origin, Higher-Order Interaction, and Stabilized Semantic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20451)
*Dimitris Vartziotis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统重构了语义场理论，将其从哲学批判发展为数学化的计算模型，包括语义场定义、高斯乘积闭包、高阶交互的Möbius反演、阶谱分析和能量最小化稳定解释，并通过三词实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将语义场理论从哲学层面的语言游戏批判发展为可独立评估的计算模型，为词汇语义学、高阶组合和稳定解释提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 提出五要素：（1）语义场模型元组；（2）高斯乘积闭包定理；（3）基于Möbius反演的任意阶语义交互分解；（4）阶谱分析度量各阶交互贡献；（5）能量泛函最小化的稳定解释框架及其存在性、下降和稳定性条件。

Result: 通过"summer day"三词实例用高斯语义场实现并验证，提供了Python代码和流程图。

Conclusion: 该理论为计算语义学提供了可操作的数学框架，但不声称完成自然语言意义理论，不取代社会、语用或规范性解释。

Abstract: Semantic Field Theory (SFT) has developed from a philosophical critique of strong anti-formalist readings of language games into a proposed computational model class for lexical semantics, higher order composition, and stabilized interpretation. This paper reconstructs that evolution and gives SFT a sharper mathematical core suitable for independent evaluation in computational linguistics and representation learning. The central proposal is that a tractable level of linguistic organization can be modeled through lexical representations expressed as semantic fields, through contextual deformation of those fields, through interaction terms defined over subsets of tokens, and through stabilization governed by semantic energy dynamics. The paper contributes five formal elements. First, it defines a semantic field model as a tuple consisting of a semantic space, a lexical field lifting, a contextual deformation map, an interaction complex, and an interpretation functional. Second, it proves a Gaussian product closure result showing that multiplicative field interactions have explicit centers, precisions, and compatibility factors. Third, it generalizes the three-word problem by using Mobius inversion on the subset lattice to isolate irreducible semantic interactions of arbitrary order. Fourth, it introduces an order spectrum that measures how much field mass is explained at each interaction order. Fifth, it formulates stabilized interpretation as minimization of an energy functional associated with the sentence and gives existence, descent, and stability conditions. A small worked example shows how a three-word summer day triple can be represented by Gaussian semantic fields, implemented in Python, and summarized by a flow diagram. The result is not a completed theory of natural language meaning and does not replace social, pragmatic, or normative accounts of language.

</details>


### [123] [A Knowledge-Injection Framework for Zero-Shot Adaptation of LLMs to Delirium Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20453)
*Jessica Sena,Shesadree Priyadarshani,Miguel Contreras,Bharat Gandhi,Scott Siegel,Subhash Nerella,Parisa Rashidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级知识注入框架，在推理时向大语言模型注入外部临床知识报告，无需微调或检索，即可提升零样本ICU谵妄预测性能。在MIMIC IV数据集上，LLaMA 8B的AUROC提升8.57个百分点，70B提升1.99个百分点，显著缩小了与GPT-5.2等前沿模型之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在临床预测任务中零样本表现有限，尤其是可本地部署的小模型缺乏领域知识；现有方法依赖微调或检索，计算成本高且不适用于资源受限场景。本文旨在探索一种轻量级、保护隐私的推理时知识注入方法，以缩小开源模型与前沿闭源模型之间的性能差距。

Method: 提出知识注入框架：在推理阶段，将结构化电子健康记录数据的确定性自然语言摘要与外部临床知识报告拼接后输入LLM，不进行微调或检索。使用LLaMA 3.1 8B和LLaMA 3.3 70B在MIMIC IV数据集的3,160次ICU入院记录上进行评估。

Result: 添加临床相关的知识报告后，8B模型AUROC提升8.57个百分点，70B模型提升1.99个百分点。与不含知识报告的GPT-5.2参考模型（AUROC 68.86%）相比，知识注入使8B模型的性能差距从15.66缩小至7.09 AUROC点，70B模型从5.30缩小至3.31 AUROC点。随机对照报告无提升甚至降低性能。SHAP归因证实注入的知识在预测中被有效利用。

Conclusion: 推理时知识注入能有效缩小可本地部署的开源模型与前沿闭源模型之间的性能差距，同时保持隐私保护的计算流程，适用于资源受限的临床环境。

Abstract: Large language models show promise for clinical prediction, but zero-shot performance on specialized tasks is limited by incomplete domain knowledge, especially for smaller locally deployable models. We present a lightweight knowledge-injection framework for zero-shot ICU delirium prediction that augments a deterministic natural-language summary of structured electronic health record data with an external clinical knowledge report at inference time, without fine-tuning or retrieval. We evaluate LLaMA 3.1 8B and LLaMA 3.3 70B on 3,160 ICU admissions from the MIMIC IV dataset. Adding a clinically meaningful external knowledge report improves AUROC by 8.57 percentage points for the 8B model and 1.99 percentage points for the 70B model compared to no external knowledge.
